2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRequires Python 3.10+. Recommended via \u003ca href=\"https://docs.astral.sh/uv/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003euv\u003c/a\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"git clone https://github.com/\u0026lt;you\u0026gt;/layercake.git\ncd layercake\nuv venv --python 3.13 .venv\nsource .venv/bin/activate\nuv pip install -r requirements.txt\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003egit clone https://github.com/\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003eyou\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e/layercake.git\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecd\u003c/span\u003e layercake\nuv venv --python 3.13 .venv\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003esource\u003c/span\u003e .venv/bin/activate\nuv pip install -r requirements.txt\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFirst run downloads SAM 2 weights (~900 MB for \u003ccode\u003esam2-hiera-large\u003c/code\u003e). Cached\nunder \u003ccode\u003e~/.cache/huggingface/\u003c/code\u003e. Device auto-detects MPS (Apple silicon),\nCUDA, or CPU.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSAM 3 text prompts (optional)\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-sam-3-text-prompts-optional\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: SAM 3 text prompts (optional)\" href=\"#sam-3-text-prompts-optional\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe Gradio UI includes an opt-in \u003cstrong\u003e\"Segment by concept\"\u003c/strong\u003e section powered by\n\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/facebookresearch/sam3\"\u003eSAM 3\u003c/a\u003e (Nov 2025). Type a short\nnoun phrase (\u003ccode\u003erope\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eface\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ehand\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eleaves\u003c/code\u003e) and SAM 3 segments \u003cstrong\u003eevery\ninstance\u003c/strong\u003e of the concept, creating one layer per instance.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis is genuinely additive to SAM 2 — SAM 2 needs you to know where to click;\nSAM 3 takes the concept name and finds it for you. Especially useful for\nscenes where the same thing repeats (multiple rope strands, a crowd, a shelf\nof objects).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOne-time setup (weights are gated):\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVisit \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/facebook/sam3\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehuggingface.co/facebook/sam3\u003c/a\u003e\nand click \u003cem\u003eAgree and access repository\u003c/em\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCreate a read-scope token at\n\u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/settings/tokens\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehuggingface.co/settings/tokens\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eexport HF_TOKEN=hf_...\u003c/code\u003e in your shell, or run \u003ccode\u003ehuggingface-cli login\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThen relaunch layercake. The first concept segmentation downloads the model\n(~3 GB) and caches it under \u003ccode\u003e~/.cache/huggingface/\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReading the scores:\u003c/strong\u003e SAM 3 multiplies each instance's confidence by an\nimage-level \"is this concept present at all\" confidence, so final scores run\nlower than you'd expect — a clearly-visible concept can land at 0.1–0.3 when\nthe presence head is conservative (thin structures like rope especially).\nIf a concept you can plainly see returns nothing, lower the score threshold\nbefore rewording the phrase; the default of 0.4 suits prominent subjects,\nnot fine ones.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eInteractive UI (recommended)\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-interactive-ui-recommended\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Interactive UI (recommended)\" href=\"#interactive-ui-recommended\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"python layers_app.py\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003epython layers_app.py\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOpens at \u003ccode\u003ehttp://127.0.0.1:7860\u003c/code\u003e. Workflow:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUpload a source image.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eName a layer (order = depth; \u003cstrong\u003efirst layer = nearest\u003c/strong\u003e) and hit \u003cstrong\u003eAdd layer\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWith the layer active, click the image. Modes:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003einclude\u003c/strong\u003e — positive click: \"this pixel belongs to the layer.\"\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eexclude\u003c/strong\u003e — negative click: \"this pixel does NOT.\"\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003emove\u003c/strong\u003e — 1st click picks up the nearest point, 2nd drops it.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ebox\u003c/strong\u003e — 2 clicks mark opposite corners of an axis-aligned bounding box.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eerase\u003c/strong\u003e — click near a point (or inside the box) to remove it.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRepeat for each layer. Edit points directly in the dataframe (move by\nchanging x/y, delete by removing rows).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eHit \u003cstrong\u003eSave layers\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOutput directory gets:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;name\u0026gt;.png\u003c/code\u003e per layer, all source-dim RGBA\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebg.png\u003c/code\u003e — the inverse of the union of all your layers\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003epoints.json\u003c/code\u003e — the full spec, for replay via the CLI\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esnippet.html\u003c/code\u003e — ready-to-paste HTML + CSS that stacks the layers\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eHeadless CLI\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-headless-cli\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Headless CLI\" href=\"#headless-cli\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSame engine, no UI. Useful in build scripts.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"python layers.py input.jpg \\\n  --layers '[\n    {\u0026quot;name\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;foreground\u0026quot;,  \u0026quot;points\u0026quot;: [[1200, 300], [850, 250]]},\n    {\u0026quot;name\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;subject\u0026quot;,     \u0026quot;points\u0026quot;: [[420, 400]], \u0026quot;box\u0026quot;: [300, 200, 600, 900]},\n    {\u0026quot;name\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;midground\u0026quot;,   \u0026quot;points\u0026quot;: [[1100, 800]], \u0026quot;labels\u0026quot;: [1]}\n  ]' \\\n  --out out/ \\\n  --edges matting \\\n  --preview\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003epython layers.py input.jpg \\\n  --layers \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e[\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e    {\"name\": \"foreground\",  \"points\": [[1200, 300], [850, 250]]},\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e    {\"name\": \"subject\",     \"points\": [[420, 400]], \"box\": [300, 200, 600, 900]},\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e    {\"name\": \"midground\",   \"points\": [[1100, 800]], \"labels\": [1]}\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e  ]\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e \\\n  --out out/ \\\n  --edges matting \\\n  --preview\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEach layer entry supports:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ename\u003c/code\u003e (required)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003epoints\u003c/code\u003e — list of \u003ccode\u003e[x, y]\u003c/code\u003e in source-image pixel space\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003elabels\u003c/code\u003e — per-point include/exclude: \u003ccode\u003e1\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e0\u003c/code\u003e (default all 1)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebox\u003c/code\u003e — optional axis-aligned \u003ccode\u003e[x1, y1, x2, y2]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003econcept\u003c/code\u003e — SAM 3 text prompt (\u003ccode\u003e\"rope\"\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e\"face\"\u003c/code\u003e); requires SAM 3 access\n(see above). A concept-only layer expands to one layer per found instance\n(capped at the 10 best after dropping near-duplicates — SAM 3 often\nreturns several overlapping instances of the same object, so an instance\nmostly covered by better-scoring ones is skipped),\nnamed \u003ccode\u003e{name}-1\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e{name}-2\u003c/code\u003e, … best-score-first,\nand the SAM 3 masks are used as-is. A concept \u003cstrong\u003ecombined with\u003c/strong\u003e points/box\ntakes the best instance as a warm-start prior and refines it with your\nprompts through SAM 2 — scripted Level 2 chaining.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003econcept_threshold\u003c/code\u003e — min score for concept instances (default \u003ccode\u003e0.4\u003c/code\u003e;\nscores are instance × presence confidence, see \"Reading the scores\" above)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003econcept_instance\u003c/code\u003e — pin the i-th best instance (1-based) instead of\nexpanding; the layer keeps its own name. UI exports use this so a\nconcept-created layer replays as the same instance.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"python layers.py input.jpg \\\n  --layers '[\n    {\u0026quot;name\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;strand\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;concept\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;rope\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;concept_threshold\u0026quot;: 0.15},\n    {\u0026quot;name\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;subject\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;concept\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;person\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;points\u0026quot;: [[420, 60]], \u0026quot;labels\u0026quot;: [0]}\n  ]' \\\n  --out out/\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003epython layers.py input.jpg \\\n  --layers \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e[\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e    {\"name\": \"strand\", \"concept\": \"rope\", \"concept_threshold\": 0.15},\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e    {\"name\": \"subject\", \"concept\": \"person\", \"points\": [[420, 60]], \"labels\": [0]}\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e  ]\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e \\\n  --out out/\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe first entry becomes \u003ccode\u003estrand-1\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estrand-2\u003c/code\u003e, … per rope instance; the\nsecond finds the best \"person\", then the negative click carves region\naround (420, 60) out of it. Concept-only layers carry no SAM 2 soft logits,\nso \u003ccode\u003e--edges sam-soft\u003c/code\u003e falls back to their hard mask edges (feather and\nmatting work normally).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--layers\u003c/code\u003e also accepts a path to a JSON file (e.g., \u003ccode\u003epoints.json\u003c/code\u003e exported\nfrom the UI). Concept-created UI layers export with their\n\u003ccode\u003econcept\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003econcept_instance\u003c/code\u003e provenance, so they replay through the CLI as\nthe same instances.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFlags\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-flags\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Flags\" href=\"#flags\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" id \u003ccode\u003efacebook/sam2-hiera-{large,base-plus,small,tiny}\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--device\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eauto\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eauto\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003ecpu\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003ecuda\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003emps\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--edges\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003efeather\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003efeather\u003c/code\u003e (Gaussian blur), \u003ccode\u003esam-soft\u003c/code\u003e (SAM's sigmoid logits), or \u003ccode\u003ematting\u003c/code\u003e (pymatting closed-form)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--feather\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e2\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBlur radius (px) for \u003ccode\u003e--edges feather\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--matting-band\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e8\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eUnknown-region band width (px) for \u003ccode\u003e--edges matting\u003c/code\u003e. Auto-narrows for masks too thin to survive erosion at this width; a mask with no interior at all keeps its hard edges\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--preview\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoff\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWrite a tinted \u003ccode\u003e_preview.png\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--no-bg\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoff\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSkip \u003ccode\u003ebg.png\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--no-depth\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoff\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSkip the Depth Anything V2 depth map\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--no-css\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoff\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSkip \u003ccode\u003esnippet.html\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--infill\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003enone\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFill the bg \"hole\" where layers sit: \u003ccode\u003enone\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eopencv\u003c/code\u003e (Navier-Stokes, instant), \u003ccode\u003elama\u003c/code\u003e (LaMa model, ~200 MB, plausible on complex scenes)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you want depth-driven parallax speeds per layer, layercake can also emit\na grayscale depth map via \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2\"\u003eDepth Anything\nV2\u003c/a\u003e. Runs alongside the\nSAM 2 pass; adds a few seconds. On by default; skip with \u003ccode\u003e--no-depth\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eEdge quality: feather vs. matting\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-edge-quality-feather-vs-matting\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Edge quality: feather vs. matting\" href=\"#edge-quality-feather-vs-matting\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003efeather\u003c/strong\u003e (default) — post-processes the hard mask with a Gaussian blur.\nFast, uniform, content-blind. Good for most cases; leaves halos on\nhigh-contrast edges and doesn't catch thin filaments.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ematting\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ca href=\"https://pymatting.github.io/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003epymatting\u003c/a\u003e alpha matting.\nBuilds a trimap (erode = known-fg, dilate-inverse = known-bg, thin band =\nunknown), then solves for soft alpha in the unknown band using actual\nimage structure. Edge-aware; dramatically better on hair, fur, and fine\nfilaments.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--matting-band\u003c/code\u003e controls the unknown-zone width: narrow (2-4) for tight\nboundaries, medium (8-12) for soft edges and hair, wide (16-24) for wispy\nstructures or motion blur.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--matting-algo\u003c/code\u003e picks the solver: \u003ccode\u003ecf\u003c/code\u003e (default, closed-form — fastest\nand highest quality on typical problems), \u003ccode\u003elbdm\u003c/code\u003e (approximation for very\nlarge unknown regions), \u003ccode\u003eknn\u003c/code\u003e (alternative kernel, sometimes useful on\nfur).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eBackground infill\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-background-infill\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Background infill\" href=\"#background-infill\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBy default \u003ccode\u003ebg.png\u003c/code\u003e has a transparent \"hole\" where your foreground layers\nsit — perfect for CSS stacking since the foreground layer sits on top and\nfills the hole visually.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you want the bg as a standalone complete image (e.g., for parallax where\nthe foreground slides and the exposed bg should look plausible), use\n\u003ccode\u003e--infill\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--infill opencv\u003c/code\u003e — OpenCV Navier-Stokes inpainting. Near-instant, zero\nextra deps beyond \u003ccode\u003eopencv-python-headless\u003c/code\u003e. Works well on simple\nbackdrops (walls, gradients, out-of-focus surfaces).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--infill lama\u003c/code\u003e — \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/advimman/lama\"\u003eLaMa\u003c/a\u003e model via\n\u003ccode\u003esimple-lama-inpainting\u003c/code\u003e. Plausible on complex scenes; first use\ndownloads ~200 MB; inference is a few seconds (CPU). Large photos are\ninpainted at the model's native ~512px scale and only the fill is\nupscaled — full-res LaMa on big holes degrades to flat mean-color mush.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOutput \u003ccode\u003ebg.png\u003c/code\u003e becomes fully opaque (alpha=1 everywhere) and contains the\ninfilled backdrop.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 layer.\u003c/strong\u003e SAM 2 hits diminishing returns\nafter ~10-15 points per blob. Split contiguous regions into separate layers\n(\u003ccode\u003ehand-left\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ehand-right\u003c/code\u003e, …) with 1-3 points each rather than 30 points\non one layer.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBoxes for the easy 80%, points for the fiddly 20%.\u003c/strong\u003e A box around a whole\nobject is often enough; add positive points for missed bits and negative\npoints to pry neighbors apart.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eClick the things you care about; let \u003ccode\u003ebg.png\u003c/code\u003e catch the rest.\u003c/strong\u003e You don't\nneed to enumerate every region.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLarge model is worth it.\u003c/strong\u003e The image encoder runs once per upload; all\nclicks after that are cheap. \u003ccode\u003esam2-hiera-large\u003c/code\u003e materially improves\nboundaries on thin structures vs. smaller variants.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCredits\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id="user-content-layercake" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: layercake" href="#layercake"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" <p dir="auto">Cut a photo into depth-ordered transparent PNG layers with <a href="https://github.com/facebookresearch/sam2">Meta's
Segment Anything 2</a>. Every output
PNG is the source image's exact dimensions, so layers stack cleanly under
CSS <code>object-fit: cover; object-position: center</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">PSD is for Photoshop. <strong>layercake is for CSS.</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">Built for layered hero composites on the web — the kind where text weaves
between a foreground object and the subject, or where parallax layers
separate fore/mid/background. Click a few points per layer, optionally draw
a bounding box, pick edge quality, save. Source dimensions guaranteed.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Why this and not something else</h2><a id="user-content-why-this-and-not-something-else" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Why this and not something else" href="#why-this-and-not-something-else"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install</h2><a id="user-content-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install" href="#install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Requires Python 3.10+. Recommended via <a href="https://docs.astral.sh/uv/" rel="nofollow">uv</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/&lt;you&gt;/layercake.git
cd layercake
uv venv --python 3.13 .venv
source .venv/bin/activate
uv pip install -r requirements.txt"><pre>git clone https://github.com/<span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>you<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>/layercake.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> layercake
uv venv --python 3.13 .venv
<span class="pl-c1">source</span> .venv/bin/activate
uv pip install -r requirements.txt</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">First run downloads SAM 2 weights (~900 MB for <code>sam2-hiera-large</code>). Cached
under <code>~/.cache/huggingface/</code>. Device auto-detects MPS (Apple silicon),
CUDA, or CPU.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">SAM 3 text prompts (optional)</h2><a id="user-content-sam-3-text-prompts-optional" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: SAM 3 text prompts (optional)" href="#sam-3-text-prompts-optional"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The Gradio UI includes an opt-in <strong>"Segment by concept"</strong> section powered by
<a href="https://github.com/facebookresearch/sam3">SAM 3</a> (Nov 2025). Type a short
noun phrase (<code>rope</code>, <code>face</code>, <code>hand</code>, <code>leaves</code>) and SAM 3 segments <strong>every
instance</strong> of the concept, creating one layer per instance.</p>
<p dir="auto">This is genuinely additive to SAM 2 — SAM 2 needs you to know where to click;
SAM 3 takes the concept name and finds it for you. Especially useful for
scenes where the same thing repeats (multiple rope strands, a crowd, a shelf
of objects).</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>One-time setup (weights are gated):</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Visit <a href="https://huggingface.co/facebook/sam3" rel="nofollow">huggingface.co/facebook/sam3</a>
and click <em>Agree and access repository</em>.</li>
<li>Create a read-scope token at
<a href="https://huggingface.co/settings/tokens" rel="nofollow">huggingface.co/settings/tokens</a>.</li>
<li><code>export HF_TOKEN=hf_...</code> in your shell, or run <code>huggingface-cli login</code>.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Then relaunch layercake. The first concept segmentation downloads the model
(~3 GB) and caches it under <code>~/.cache/huggingface/</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Reading the scores:</strong> SAM 3 multiplies each instance's confidence by an
image-level "is this concept present at all" confidence, so final scores run
lower than you'd expect — a clearly-visible concept can land at 0.1–0.3 when
the presence head is conservative (thin structures like rope especially).
If a concept you can plainly see returns nothing, lower the score threshold
before rewording the phrase; the default of 0.4 suits prominent subjects,
not fine ones.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Interactive UI (recommended)</h2><a id="user-content-interactive-ui-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Interactive UI (recommended)" href="#interactive-ui-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python layers_app.py"><pre>python layers_app.py</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Opens at <code>http://127.0.0.1:7860</code>. Workflow:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Upload a source image.</li>
<li>Name a layer (order = depth; <strong>first layer = nearest</strong>) and hit <strong>Add layer</strong>.</li>
<li>With the layer active, click the image. Modes:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>include</strong> — positive click: "this pixel belongs to the layer."</li>
<li><strong>exclude</strong> — negative click: "this pixel does NOT."</li>
<li><strong>move</strong> — 1st click picks up the nearest point, 2nd drops it.</li>
<li><strong>box</strong> — 2 clicks mark opposite corners of an axis-aligned bounding box.</li>
<li><strong>erase</strong> — click near a point (or inside the box) to remove it.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Repeat for each layer. Edit points directly in the dataframe (move by
changing x/y, delete by removing rows).</li>
<li>Hit <strong>Save layers</strong>.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Output directory gets:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>&lt;name&gt;.png</code> per layer, all source-dim RGBA</li>
<li><code>bg.png</code> — the inverse of the union of all your layers</li>
<li><code>points.json</code> — the full spec, for replay via the CLI</li>
<li><code>snippet.html</code> — ready-to-paste HTML + CSS that stacks the layers</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Headless CLI</h2><a id="user-content-headless-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Headless CLI" href="#headless-cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Same engine, no UI. Useful in build scripts.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python layers.py input.jpg \
  --layers '[
    {&quot;name&quot;: &quot;foreground&quot;,  &quot;points&quot;: [[1200, 300], [850, 250]]},
    {&quot;name&quot;: &quot;subject&quot;,     &quot;points&quot;: [[420, 400]], &quot;box&quot;: [300, 200, 600, 900]},
    {&quot;name&quot;: &quot;midground&quot;,   &quot;points&quot;: [[1100, 800]], &quot;labels&quot;: [1]}
  ]' \
  --out out/ \
  --edges matting \
  --preview"><pre>python layers.py input.jpg \
  --layers <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>[</span>
<span class="pl-s">    {"name": "foreground",  "points": [[1200, 300], [850, 250]]},</span>
<span class="pl-s">    {"name": "subject",     "points": [[420, 400]], "box": [300, 200, 600, 900]},</span>
<span class="pl-s">    {"name": "midground",   "points": [[1100, 800]], "labels": [1]}</span>
<span class="pl-s">  ]<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> \
  --out out/ \
  --edges matting \
  --preview</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Each layer entry supports:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>name</code> (required)</li>
<li><code>points</code> — list of <code>[x, y]</code> in source-image pixel space</li>
<li><code>labels</code> — per-point include/exclude: <code>1</code> or <code>0</code> (default all 1)</li>
<li><code>box</code> — optional axis-aligned <code>[x1, y1, x2, y2]</code></li>
<li><code>concept</code> — SAM 3 text prompt (<code>"rope"</code>, <code>"face"</code>); requires SAM 3 access
(see above). A concept-only layer expands to one layer per found instance
(capped at the 10 best after dropping near-duplicates — SAM 3 often
returns several overlapping instances of the same object, so an instance
mostly covered by better-scoring ones is skipped),
named <code>{name}-1</code>, <code>{name}-2</code>, … best-score-first,
and the SAM 3 masks are used as-is. A concept <strong>combined with</strong> points/box
takes the best instance as a warm-start prior and refines it with your
prompts through SAM 2 — scripted Level 2 chaining.</li>
<li><code>concept_threshold</code> — min score for concept instances (default <code>0.4</code>;
scores are instance × presence confidence, see "Reading the scores" above)</li>
<li><code>concept_instance</code> — pin the i-th best instance (1-based) instead of
expanding; the layer keeps its own name. UI exports use this so a
concept-created layer replays as the same instance.</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python layers.py input.jpg \
  --layers '[
    {&quot;name&quot;: &quot;strand&quot;, &quot;concept&quot;: &quot;rope&quot;, &quot;concept_threshold&quot;: 0.15},
    {&quot;name&quot;: &quot;subject&quot;, &quot;concept&quot;: &quot;person&quot;, &quot;points&quot;: [[420, 60]], &quot;labels&quot;: [0]}
  ]' \
  --out out/"><pre>python layers.py input.jpg \
  --layers <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>[</span>
<span class="pl-s">    {"name": "strand", "concept": "rope", "concept_threshold": 0.15},</span>
<span class="pl-s">    {"name": "subject", "concept": "person", "points": [[420, 60]], "labels": [0]}</span>
<span class="pl-s">  ]<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> \
  --out out/</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The first entry becomes <code>strand-1</code>, <code>strand-2</code>, … per rope instance; the
second finds the best "person", then the negative click carves region
around (420, 60) out of it. Concept-only layers carry no SAM 2 soft logits,
so <code>--edges sam-soft</code> falls back to their hard mask edges (feather and
matting work normally).</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>--layers</code> also accepts a path to a JSON file (e.g., <code>points.json</code> exported
from the UI). Concept-created UI layers export with their
<code>concept</code>/<code>concept_instance</code> provenance, so they replay through the CLI as
the same instances.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Flags</h3><a id="user-content-flags" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Flags" href="#flags"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Flag</th>
<th>Default</th>
<th>What</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>--model</code></td>
<td><code>sam2-hiera-large</code></td>
<td>HF id <code>facebook/sam2-hiera-{large,base-plus,small,tiny}</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--device</code></td>
<td><code>auto</code></td>
<td><code>auto</code>/<code>cpu</code>/<code>cuda</code>/<code>mps</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--edges</code></td>
<td><code>feather</code></td>
<td><code>feather</code> (Gaussian blur), <code>sam-soft</code> (SAM's sigmoid logits), or <code>matting</code> (pymatting closed-form)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--feather</code></td>
<td><code>2</code></td>
<td>Blur radius (px) for <code>--edges feather</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--matting-band</code></td>
<td><code>8</code></td>
<td>Unknown-region band width (px) for <code>--edges matting</code>. Auto-narrows for masks too thin to survive erosion at this width; a mask with no interior at all keeps its hard edges</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--preview</code></td>
<td>off</td>
<td>Write a tinted <code>_preview.png</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--no-bg</code></td>
<td>off</td>
<td>Skip <code>bg.png</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--no-depth</code></td>
<td>off</td>
<td>Skip the Depth Anything V2 depth map</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--no-css</code></td>
<td>off</td>
<td>Skip <code>snippet.html</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>--infill</code></td>
<td><code>none</code></td>
<td>Fill the bg "hole" where layers sit: <code>none</code>, <code>opencv</code> (Navier-Stokes, instant), <code>lama</code> (LaMa model, ~200 MB, plausible on complex scenes)</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Depth map (optional)</h3><a id="user-content-depth-map-optional" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Depth map (optional)" href="#depth-map-optional"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you want depth-driven parallax speeds per layer, layercake can also emit
a grayscale depth map via <a href="https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2">Depth Anything
V2</a>. Runs alongside the
SAM 2 pass; adds a few seconds. On by default; skip with <code>--no-depth</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Edge quality: feather vs. matting</h2><a id="user-content-edge-quality-feather-vs-matting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Edge quality: feather vs. matting" href="#edge-quality-feather-vs-matting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>feather</strong> (default) — post-processes the hard mask with a Gaussian blur.
Fast, uniform, content-blind. Good for most cases; leaves halos on
high-contrast edges and doesn't catch thin filaments.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>matting</strong> — <a href="https://pymatting.github.io/" rel="nofollow">pymatting</a> alpha matting.
Builds a trimap (erode = known-fg, dilate-inverse = known-bg, thin band =
unknown), then solves for soft alpha in the unknown band using actual
image structure. Edge-aware; dramatically better on hair, fur, and fine
filaments.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>--matting-band</code> controls the unknown-zone width: narrow (2-4) for tight
boundaries, medium (8-12) for soft edges and hair, wide (16-24) for wispy
structures or motion blur.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>--matting-algo</code> picks the solver: <code>cf</code> (default, closed-form — fastest
and highest quality on typical problems), <code>lbdm</code> (approximation for very
large unknown regions), <code>knn</code> (alternative kernel, sometimes useful on
fur).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Background infill</h2><a id="user-content-background-infill" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Background infill" href="#background-infill"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default <code>bg.png</code> has a transparent "hole" where your foreground layers
sit — perfect for CSS stacking since the foreground layer sits on top and
fills the hole visually.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you want the bg as a standalone complete image (e.g., for parallax where
the foreground slides and the exposed bg should look plausible), use
<code>--infill</code>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>--infill opencv</code> — OpenCV Navier-Stokes inpainting. Near-instant, zero
extra deps beyond <code>opencv-python-headless</code>. Works well on simple
backdrops (walls, gradients, out-of-focus surfaces).</li>
<li><code>--infill lama</code> — <a href="https://github.com/advimman/lama">LaMa</a> model via
<code>simple-lama-inpainting</code>. Plausible on complex scenes; first use
downloads ~200 MB; inference is a few seconds (CPU). Large photos are
inpainted at the model's native ~512px scale and only the fill is
upscaled — full-res LaMa on big holes degrades to flat mean-color mush.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Output <code>bg.png</code> becomes fully opaque (alpha=1 everywhere) and contains the
infilled backdrop.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CSS integration</h2><a id="user-content-css-integration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CSS integration" href="#css-integration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>snippet.html</code> ships ready to paste:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;hero&quot;&gt;
  &lt;img src=&quot;images/bg.png&quot; class=&quot;layer l-bg&quot; alt=&quot;&quot;&gt;
  &lt;h1 class=&quot;hero-wordmark&quot;&gt;your wordmark&lt;/h1&gt;
  &lt;img src=&quot;images/subject.png&quot; class=&quot;layer l-subject&quot; alt=&quot;&quot;&gt;
  &lt;img src=&quot;images/foreground.png&quot; class=&quot;layer l-foreground&quot; alt=&quot;&quot;&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">hero</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">img</span> <span class="pl-c1">src</span>="<span class="pl-s">images/bg.png</span>" <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">layer l-bg</span>" <span class="pl-c1">alt</span>=""<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">h1</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">hero-wordmark</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>your wordmark<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">h1</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">img</span> <span class="pl-c1">src</span>="<span class="pl-s">images/subject.png</span>" <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">layer l-subject</span>" <span class="pl-c1">alt</span>=""<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">img</span> <span class="pl-c1">src</span>="<span class="pl-s">images/foreground.png</span>" <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">layer l-foreground</span>" <span class="pl-c1">alt</span>=""<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=".hero { position: relative; isolation: isolate; overflow: hidden; }
.hero .layer { position: absolute; inset: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%;
               object-fit: cover; object-position: center;
               pointer-events: none; }
.hero .l-bg { z-index: 1; }
.hero .hero-wordmark { z-index: 2; position: relative; /* your styles */ }
.hero .l-subject { z-index: 3; }
.hero .l-foreground { z-index: 4; }"><pre>.<span class="pl-c1">hero</span> { <span class="pl-c1">position</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> relative; <span class="pl-c1">isolation</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> isolate; <span class="pl-c1">overflow</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> hidden; }
.<span class="pl-c1">hero</span> .<span class="pl-c1">layer</span> { <span class="pl-c1">position</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> absolute; <span class="pl-c1">inset</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>; <span class="pl-c1">width</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">100<span class="pl-smi">%</span></span>; <span class="pl-c1">height</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">100<span class="pl-smi">%</span></span>;
               <span class="pl-c1">object-fit</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> cover; <span class="pl-c1">object-position</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> center;
               <span class="pl-c1">pointer-events</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> none; }
.<span class="pl-c1">hero</span> .<span class="pl-c1">l-bg</span> { <span class="pl-c1">z-index</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span>; }
.<span class="pl-c1">hero</span> .<span class="pl-c1">hero-wordmark</span> { <span class="pl-c1">z-index</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span>; <span class="pl-c1">position</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> relative; <span class="pl-c">/* your styles */</span> }
.<span class="pl-c1">hero</span> .<span class="pl-c1">l-subject</span> { <span class="pl-c1">z-index</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">3</span>; }
.<span class="pl-c1">hero</span> .<span class="pl-c1">l-foreground</span> { <span class="pl-c1">z-index</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">4</span>; }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The wordmark sits between the background and the subject — text weaves
through the composite naturally.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Workflow tips</h2><a id="user-content-workflow-tips" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Workflow tips" href="#workflow-tips"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Many small layers beats one big layer.</strong> SAM 2 hits diminishing returns
after ~10-15 points per blob. Split contiguous regions into separate layers
(<code>hand-left</code>, <code>hand-right</code>, …) with 1-3 points each rather than 30 points
on one layer.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Boxes for the easy 80%, points for the fiddly 20%.</strong> A box around a whole
object is often enough; add positive points for missed bits and negative
points to pry neighbors apart.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Click the things you care about; let <code>bg.png</code> catch the rest.</strong> You don't
need to enumerate every region.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Large model is worth it.</strong> The image encoder runs once per upload; all
clicks after that are cheap. <code>sam2-hiera-large</code> materially improves
boundaries on thin structures vs. smaller variants.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Credits</h2><a id="user-content-credits" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Credits" href="#credits"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Built on top of:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/facebookresearch/sam2">Segment Anything 2</a> (Meta)</li>
<li><a href="https://pymatting.github.io/" rel="nofollow">pymatting</a> (closed-form alpha matting)</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2">Depth Anything V2</a></li>
<li><a href="https://gradio.app" rel="nofollow">Gradio</a> for the UI</li>
</ul>
